ceyyum eṉ eccamē muṟṟu ātal -
‘muṟṟēl’ eṉavē, ceyyum eṉ eccam muṟṟum ām eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. muṟṟuvikutiyiṉṟi eccavikutiyē muṟṟuvikutip poruḷākiya pāl kāṭṭi orōvaḻi niṟṟaliṉ, eccam ‘muṟṟām’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
e-ṭu :	‘ām poruḷkaḷ ākum,’ ‘pōm poruḷkaḷ pōkum’ (cīvaka. 848), vām puravi)	(naṉ. 341 caṅ.)
‘ceyyum eṉ ecca īṟṟuuyirmey cēṟalum’ eṉṉum cūttirattu ‘muṟṟēl’ eṉavē, ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccam muṟṟu viṉiyu mām eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. eccam muṟṟām eṉavē, iccūttiram eytātatu eytuvittal āyiṟṟu.     (naṉ. 341 irāmā.)
ceyyum eṉpataṉ ciṟappuviti -
ceyyum eṉ eccattiṉ īṟṟayal uyirmey keṭutalum, ceyyum eṉpatu ceyyuḷil ceyyuntu eṉa īṟṟu ‘um’ untu ākalum, ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟiṉ īṟṟayal uyirēṉum uyirmeyyēṉum keṭutalum orōvaḻi uḷavām.
e-ṭu :	vāvum puravi - vām puravi; pōkum puḻi - pōm puḻi: īṟṟayal uyirmey keṭṭatu. pāyum - ‘pāyuntu’; kūppeyarkkum - ‘kūppeyarkkuntu’ (puṟanā. 24, 395): ivi um ‘untu’ āyiṉa.
kaluḻumē - kaluḻmē - īṟṟayal ukaravuyir keṭṭatu.
moḻiyumē - moḻimē (kuṟun. 51) - īṟṟayal uyirmey keṭṭatu.
vitavātu potuppaṭa ‘muṟṟēl’ eṉṟamiyāṉ, kēḷum - kēṇmō, uriyum - urimō - eṉa muṉṉilip paṉmi ēvalmuṟṟukkaḷ muṟiyē īṟṟayal uyirum uyirmeyyum keṭutalum koḷḷappaṭum. puṟanaṭivitiyāṉ, ‘āṭuvāmō’ eṉṉum taṉmippaṉmimuṟṟu, etirkāla vakariṭinili keṭṭu ‘āṭāmō’ eṉa vantamiyum koḷka. 	(tiruvā. tiruppoṉ 1-9)  (naṉ. 341 caṅ.)
ceyyum eṉpatu tiriyumāṟu -
ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccamum uṇṭu; viṉimuṟṟum uṇṭu. ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccam īṟṟayal eḻuttākiya ukaram ēṟiya meykeṭṭu muṭitalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	vāvum puravi - vām puravi; pōkum puḻi - pōm puḻi
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟiṉ īṟṟayal eḻuttākiya ukaram tāṉ ēṟiya meyyoṭum keṭutalumuṇṭu; tāṉ māttiram keṭutalu muṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	moḻiyumē - moḻimē; (kuṟun.51) kaluḻumē - kaluḻmē eṉa, muṟiyē kāṇka.				(to. co. 240 nac. uri)
muṭimē, polimē eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷil vantuḷḷaṉa.
ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccam -
peyarecca vāypāṭukaḷ iraṇṭaṉuḷ iḵtu oṉṟu. ēṉiya ceyta eṉṉum peyareccam iṟantakālam uṇart tavum, icceyyum eṉṉum peyareccam nikaḻkālam etirkālam eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉkaṇṇum varuvatu. ceyyum eṉpatum ceyta eṉpatu pōla, nilam poruḷ kālam karuvi viṉimutal toḻil - eṉṉum aṟuvakip peyaroṭum muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	vāḻum il , kaṟkum nūl, tuyilum kālam, vaṉiyum kōl, ōtum pārppāṉ, uṇṇum ūṇ - eṉa āṟu poruṭkum urittāy vantavāṟu.
nōy tīrum maruntu - nōy tīrtaṟkuk kāraṇamāya maruntu - eṉa varum kāraṇam karuvikkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
niṉmukam kāṇum maruntu - niṉmukattik kāṇṭal kāraṇa māka ataṉ kāriyamākap piṟanta aruḷ maruntātal taṉmiyatu ātaliṉ kāriyattiṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
aracaṉ ā koṭukkum pārppāṉ - ‘iṉṉataṟku’ eṉṟa poruḷil vantatu.
āṭi olikkum kūli - ‘itupayaṉ’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
paḻam utirum kōṭu - nīṅkaṟporuṇmi (itaṉic cēṉā variyar ellipporuṇmiyil aṭakkuvar)			(to. co. 236 nac. uri)
ceyyum eṉṉum peyareñcukiḷavikkum īṟṟumici ukaram keṭutal -
ceyyum eṉṉum peyareñcukiḷavikkum, ummiyāl ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟuccoṟkum īṟṟayal ukaram tāṉ ēṟiya meyyoṭum keṭum; (ummiyāl) meyyoḻittut tāṉ māttiramē keṭum.
e-ṭu :	‘vāam puravi vaḻuti’ : vāvum ‘vām’ eṉa niṉṟatu - ceyyum eṉ peyareccam. ‘ampal ūrum avaṉoṭu moḻimē’ (kuṟun. 51) moḻiyumē ‘moḻimē’ eṉa niṉṟatu - ceyyum eṉmuṟṟu.
	‘cāral nāṭeṉ tōḻiyum kaluḻmē’ : ceyyum eṉ muṟṟiṉ īṟṟayal ukaruyir māttiram keṭṭatu.
meyyoḻittu ukaram māttiram keṭutal ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟuccoṟkē eṉak koḷka.	(to. co. 232 tey. uri)
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu -
itu nikaḻkālamuṟṟuccoṟkaḷil oṉṟu. iḵtu irutiṇip potuviṉi. paṭarkkiyil palarpāl, taṉmi muṉṉili ivaṟṟic cārnta peyarkaḷukku itu muṭikkum collāka vārātu; paṭarkki āṇ peṇ oṉṟu pala - eṉṉum nāṟpālilēyē varum.
e-ṭu :	avaṉ varum, avaḷ varum, atu varum, avi varum.
	(to. co. 229 nac. uri)
mukkālattiṉum nikaḻum poruḷkaḷiyum ceyyum eṉṉum  vāypāṭṭāṉ kiḷattalē muṟi, nikaḻkālattil mukkālamum aṭaṅkutaliṉ.
e-ṭu :	mali niṟkum, tīc cuṭum, ñāyiṟu iyaṅkum, tiṅkaḷ iyaṅkum.	(to. co. 242 nac. uri)
uyartiṇippaṉmip paṭarkkiyilum muṉṉiliyilum taṉmiyilum cellātu, ceyyum eṉ eccattāṉ ākum ceyyu meṉṉum muṟṟu. ātaliṉ paṭarkki āṇ peṇ oṉṟu pala eṉṉum nāṉku pālkaḷilēyē ivvāypāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟu nikaḻum.
e-ṭu :	avaṉ uṇṇum, avaḷ uṇṇum, atu uṇṇum, avi uṇṇum.
ēṉi muṟṟukkaḷ pōla immuṟṟut taṉ iyalpil nillātu, eccattāṉ ākiyatu eṉṟār.	(naṉ. 348 caṅ.)
uṇṇum tiṉṉum eṉṟal toṭakkattu  muṉṉilip paṉmikkaṇ varum um īṟṟu (ēval) muṟṟukkaḷ muṉ ṉilikkaṇ putuvatu pukutalām. im muṟṟukkaḷum ceyyum eṉ muṟṟup pōla īṟṟayaleḻuttuk keṭṭu varutalumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	‘tēvirkāḷ emmuṟu nōy tīrm’ (cilap. 9 : 14) - īṟṟayal uyir keṭṭatu. ‘cilampuḷa koṇm’ (cilap. 9:73) - īṟṟayal uyirmey (ḷu) keṭṭatu.  ‘mantira nāmam vantunīr kaṉm’ (peruṅ 2: 11 : 94) - īṟṟayal uyirmey (lu) keṭṭatu 
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu aḵṟiṇiorumi cuṭṭutal -
cāttaṉ kurikkum, cātti pul mēyum - eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa ciṟappu viṉikaḷāl cāttaṉ cātti - eṉṟa potup peyarkaḷ aḵṟiṇi orumiccoṟkaḷ āyavāṟu.			(to. co. 169 tey. uri)
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu uyartiṇi orumi cuṭṭutal -
cāttaṉ yāḻ eḻūum, cātti cāntarikkum - eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa ciṟappuviṉikaḷāl cāttaṉ cātti eṉṟa potup peyarkaḷ uyartiṇi orumiccoṟkaḷ āyavāṟu.			(to. co. 173 cēṉā. uri)
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟum ceyyum eṉṉum eccamum -
ceyyum eṉpatu muṟṟām nilimikkaṇ pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmi oḻittu oḻinta nāṉku pāliyum taṉ īṟṟakattē oruvāṟṟāṉ koṇṭa appeya rāṉē amintu muṭiyum. aḵtu eccamāya  nilimikkaṇ muṭipāka varum peyar payaṉili ceppalum urupēṟṟalum uṭiyavām.	(to. co. 230 iḷa. uri)
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu, paṭarkki āṇ peṇ oṉṟu pala - eṉṉum nāṉku pāṟkē varum; pallōrpaṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmiyil vārātu. ceyyum eṉṉum eccam irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭattum varum. muṟṟāyp peyar koṇṭavaḻi maṟṟoru col nōkkātu, ceppu mūṭiyak kāl pōla, amintumāṟum. eccamāyp peyar koṇṭak kāl amiyātu maṟṟumoru col nōkkiṟṟup pōla niṟkum. iṉi muṟṟāyavaḻi ‘uṇṇum’ eṉa ūṉṟiṉāṟ pōla nalintu collappaṭum eṉṟum, eccamāyavaḻi ūṉṟātu nekiḻa muṭipu collappaṭum eṉṟum koḷka.		(to. co. 236 kal. uri)
ceyyum eṉṉum viṉi viḷakkam -
ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiccol pala nili kaḷil niṟkum
1. avaṉ varum, avaḷ varum, atu varum, avi varum -potumuṟṟu. 2. ceyyum cāttaṉ - peyareccam. 3. avaṉ iṉṟu ceyyum - nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟu. 4. avaṉ nāḷic ceyyum - etirkāla viṉimuṟṟu. 5. nīyir uṇṇum - muṉṉili uyartiṇippaṉmic ciṟappumuṟṟu. 6. tōḻiyum kaluḻm - kaluḻum eṉṉum muṟṟiṉ īṟṟayal ukaram keṭṭatu. 7. ‘micip pāyuntu miḻali’ - pāyum eṉṉum umīṟu. ‘untu’ āyiṟṟu.
5. muṉṉilippaṉmiyil um īṟṟuccol ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu muṟṟu aṉṟu eṉpatē nacciṉārk kiṉiyar karuttu (to. co. 226). (ilakkaṇak kottil 65, 66, 67 ām nūṟpākkaḷ piṟaṉkōḷ kūṟum potucceyti; ivvāciriyaratu karuttu eṉakkōṭal kūṭātu.)	(i. ko. 67)
ceyyum viṉimutal -
taccaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa viyam, kaṭavuḷāl ākkappaṭṭa vimāṉam, puliyāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭāṉ, cuṟavāl ēṟuṇṭāṉ  (ālurupu ēṟṟa taccaṉ mutaliya) ivi ceyyum viṉimutal.		(naṉ. 296 mayili.)
ceyyuḷ āvatu -
pal vakippaṭṭa tātukkaḷiṉāṉ uyirkku iṭaṉāka iyaṟ ṟappaṭṭa uṭampu pōla, palavakic coṟkaḷāṉum poru ḷukku iṭaṉākat  taṅkaḷ aṟiviṉāṉ kaṟṟuvallōr alaṅkāram tōṉṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷām.			(naṉ. 267 mayili.)
ceyyuḷ īṭṭaccol -
centamiḻ nāṭṭil pēccuvaḻakkil payilum iyaṟcol, perumpāṉmiyum ceyyuḷukku vaḻaṅkappaṭum tiricol, centamiḻnāṭṭic cērnta paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nilattilum payiṉṟu centamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ticiccol, āriyaccol tamiḻolikku ēṟpat tirittuk koḷḷap paṭum vaṭacol - eṉṉum nālvakic collum coṟkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērtalāṉ amiyum ceyyuḷi amittaṟku utavum coṟkaḷām.			(to. co. 397 cēṉā. uri)
ceyyuḷmuṭipum viṉittiricollum -
eḻuttatikārattē tolkāppiyaṉār ‘ceyyuṭku iv vāṟu muṭikka’ eṉṟu muṭipu kūṟiyavaṟṟi yellām ceyyuḷmuṭipu eṉṟu kūṟalum (eḻut. 288, 356), kēṭṭī vāyāyiṉ - ceppīmaṉ - īṅku vantīttāy - pukaḻntikum allarō - eṉ maṉār - eṉṟiciṉōr - peṟalaruṅkurittu ( kēṭpāyāyiṉ, ceppu, īṅku vantāy, pukaḻntārallarō, eṉpa, eṉṟaṉar, peṟalaritu - eṉa muṟiyē poruḷpaṭum.) eṉpaṉa mutalāka ivvāṟu tirintu vantaṉavaṟṟi viṉit tiricol eṉṟu kūṟalum tolkāppiyaṉār karuttām.		(to. co. 399 nac. uri)
ceyyuḷvikārattāṉ moḻi varuvittu muṭittal -
moḻi varuvittu muṭittal pala ētukkaḷāṉ nikaḻum. avaṟṟuḷ ceyyuḷvikāramum oṉṟu.
‘ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟē’  	(to.e. 221)
‘ceyta ceykiṉṟa ceyyumeṉ pāṭṭil’  	(naṉ. 340)
‘karuttup patapporuḷ kāṭṭu mūṉṟiṉum’   	(22)
‘paḻiyañcip pāttūṇ uṭittāyiṉ vāḻkki’   	(ku. 44)
‘tuṇivaliyum tūkkic ceyal’  	(ku. 471)
ivaṟṟuḷ ‘akariṟuti iyaṟṟē’, ‘ceyyumeṉ vāypāṭ ṭil,’ ‘eṭuttukkāṭṭu’, ‘ilvāḻkki’, ‘cīrtūkkic ceyal’ - eṉa vikārattāl tokkavaṟṟi varuvittu muṭitta vāṟu.	(i. ko. 89.)
ceyyuḷvikāram āṟu -
‘valittal melittal nīṭṭal kuṟukkal virittal tokuttal - eṉṟu āṟum ceyyuḷvikāramām. melleḻutti valleḻut tākkalum, valleḻutti melleḻuttākka lum, kuṟṟeḻutti nīṭṭalum, neṭṭeḻuttik kuṟukka lum, ōreḻutti virittalum, ōreḻuttit tokuttalum eṉpaṉa muṟiyē avvikāraṅkaḷ.			(to. co. 403 cēṉā. uri)
ceyyuḷil eṭuttukkāṭṭuc cilavē tantami -
“ātaṉ tanti eṉpatu ceyyuḷil ānti eṉṟē varum. itaṉi vēṟṟumittokikku utāraṇam kāṭṭiṉāl, tolkāppiyam kallātavarum ciṟunūlkaḷē kaṟṟavarum itaṉi aṟintukoḷḷa iyalāmal (nilimoḻi ‘taṉ’ īṟum, varumoḻiyuḷ takara uyirmeyyum keṭṭu muṭintami teriyāmal) paṟavip peyarō piḻiyō eṉak kalaṅkuvar. ‘piṇikkaṇ varuntiṉāṉ’ eṉpatu urupu tokkuc ceyyuḷil variṉ ikkuccāriyi peṟṟup ‘piṇikku varuntiṉāṉ’ eṉavē varum. itaṉi vēṟṟumit tokikku utāraṇa mākak koṭuttāl utāraṇamum piḻi eṉpar. ākavē ceyyuḷvaḻakkuc cilavum ulaka vaḻakkup palavum tantōm” eṉṟu ilakkaṇakkottu āciriyar viḷakkukiṟār.			(i. ko. 12)
ceyyuḷukkuriya pala col -
poruḷāti aṟuvakip peyarkaḷuḷ oruporuḷ kuṟitta palacollākiyum, palaporuḷ kuṟitta orucollākiyum varum peyarttiricoṟkaḷum, pavvīṟum mārīṟumāṉa paṭarkki muṟṟum, al aṉ eṉ ēṉ ku ṭu tu ṟu am ām em ēm kum ṭum tum ṟum eṉṉum īṟṟut taṉmiorumiyum paṉmiyumāṉa muṟṟum, aiyum iyyum miṉṉum īṟṟu muṉṉili muṟṟum, kakāra yakāra rakāra īṟṟu viyaṅkōḷ muṟṟum, ceytu ceypu ceyyā ceyyū ceyteṉa ceya ceyiṉ ceyyiya ceyyiyar vāṉ pāṉ pākku eṉṉum viṉiyeccamum, oḻinta īṟukaḷāṉ varum viṉittiricoṟ kaḷum, iṭit tiricoṟkaḷum, urittiri coṟkaḷum, āṉum - oṭuvum - iṉṉum - ātuvum - avvum - piṉ muṉ il eṉṉum mūṉṟum oḻittu oḻinta kaṇ mutalāṉa ellā ēḻaṉurupum - āṉa ivvurupu ēṟṟa moḻikaḷum, valittal mutalāṉa vikāra moḻikaḷum, tokinili moḻikaḷum, ‘āṭā aṭaku’ (tiṇimā.4) ‘tīttīṇṭu kiyār’ (tiṇamā.5) - eṉpaṉa mutalāṉa kuṟippumoḻikaḷum, paṟavāk kuḷavi - pāyā vēṅki - mutalāṉa veḷippaṭi moḻi kaḷum, mūṉṟu iṟanta aṭukkumoḻikaḷum, eḻuttatikārat tuḷ ceyyuṭku eṉṟu vitantōtiyaṉavum mutalāṉa ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷum, ticiccoṟkaḷum, vaṭacoṟkaḷum ām. (āṭāaṭaku - makaḷir viḷiyāṭṭu) 	(naṉ. 267 mayili.)
ceyvatu ilvaḻi nikaḻkālam kāṭṭal -
mikkatu eṉṟu naṉku matikkappaṭum poruṭkaṇ nikaḻum viṉiyiṉ payaṉākiya kuṇattik kuṟitta eḻuvāy, ceyki muṭiyāta nilikkaṇ nikaḻkālattāl collappaṭum. 
e-ṭu :	aṟam ceytāṉ cuvarkkam pukum.
aṟam eṉpatu mikkatu. ataṉi ākkiṉāṉ cuvarkkam pukutal orutaliyātaliṉ pukukiṉṟārik kaṇṭāṉ pōlap ‘pukum’ eṉṟu nikaḻkālattāṉ colla amiyum eṉpatu. ceyvatu eṉpatu ceyki; ilvaḻi - muṭiyāta nilimi.			(to. co. 238 iḷa. uri)
ceyvi eṉ viṉiyum ceyviṉi -
piṟaviṉiyum orōviṭattut taṉviṉipporuḷē tarum.
e-ṭu :	‘naṭpāṭal tēṟṟā tavar’ ku. 187 ‘tēṟṟā oḻukkam oruvaṉkaṇ uṇṭāyiṉ’ 	nālaṭi. 75
ivaṟṟuḷ, tēṟātavar - tēṟā oḻukkam - eṉat taṉviṉi piṟaviṉi pōla niṉṟaṉa. ‘pōkkum atuviḷin taṟṟu’ (ku. 332) - pōtalum eṉṟu taṉviṉi yākavē poruḷpaṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. ‘kivēl kaḷiṟṟoṭu pōkki’ (ku.774) - makaṭ pōkkiya tāy - eṉa varutaliṉ, ‘pōkku’ piṟaviṉiyēyām.	(i. ko. 72)
sivaṟṟip pakutipporuḷ vikuti eṉavum, cuvārttattil vanta kāritam eṉṟum pi. vi. kūṟum. 	(kāriki 35)]
ceyviṉi ceyappāṭṭuviṉi :  ivaṟṟiṉ 
potuvākum toṭarviṉi -
uṇṭa cāttaṉ : ceyviṉip peyareccam; itu ceyap pāṭṭu viṉip poruḷpaṭātu. uṇṭa cōṟu - uṇṇap paṭṭa cōṟu; itu ceyappāṭṭuviṉip peyareccam.
pulikoṉṟa yāṉi eṉpuḻi, puliyik koṉṟa yāṉi eṉiṉ ceyviṉiyākavum, puliyāl kollappaṭṭa yāṉi eṉiṉ ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyākavum peyareccam poruḷ paṭum.
mīṉ viḻuṅkiṉavaṉ, avaṉ virumpiṉavaṉ, oṉṉār vaṇaṅki ṉāṉ, aṟintu vantāṉ :  ivi, mīṉi viḻuṅ kiṉavaṉ - mīṉāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭavaṉ - eṉavum, avaṉi virumpiṉa vaṉ - avaṉāl virumpappaṭṭavaṉ - eṉavum, oṉṉāri vaṇaṅkiṉavaṉ - oṉṉārāl vaṇaṅkappaṭṭavaṉ - eṉavum, tāṉ aṟintu vantavaṉ - aṟiyappaṭṭu vantavaṉ - eṉavum mutal mūṉṟu muṟṟuppeyarkaḷum (viṉiyāla ṇiyum peyarkaḷum) ceyviṉi ceyappāṭṭuviṉi eṉum iraṇ ṭaṟkum potuvāyiṉavāṟum, iṟutiyatākiya viṉiyecca mum avvāṟē potuvāyiṉavāṟum kaṇṭu koḷka.	(i. ko. 67)
ceya eṉ eccam -
ceya eṉ eccam, maḻi peyya eḻuntatu eṉa etirkālattum, maḻi peyyak kuḷam niṟintatu - eṉa iṟantakālattum varum. maḻi peyyak kuḷam niṟintatu - eṉak kāraṇap poruṭ ṭāyum, kuḷam niṟiya maḻi peytatu - eṉak kāriyap poruṭṭāyum, maḻi peyya eḻuntatu eṉa ‘ataṟ poruṭṭu’ eṉṉum poruṭṭāyum, maḻi peyyac cāttaṉ vantāṉ - eṉa uṭaṉikaḻcciyāy nikaḻttaṟkaṇ iṭap poruṭṭāyum piṟavāṟāyum varum.	(to. co. 230 kal. uri)
ceyappaṭuporuḷ -
eḻuvāyāṉ ceyyappaṭum toḻiliṉ payaṉi uṟuvatu ceyappaṭuporuḷām. iḵtu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip poruḷāka varum. ipporuḷ, iyaṟṟappaṭutal - vēṟu paṭukkappaṭutal - eytappaṭutal - eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
iyaṟṟappaṭutal - muṉ illataṉi uṇṭākkutal : eyili iḻittāṉ ; vēṟupaṭukkappaṭutal - muṉ uḷḷataṉit tirittal : maratti aṟuttāṉ; eytappaṭutal - iyaṟṟalum vēṟupaṭuttalumiṉṟic ceyappaṭuporuḷ toḻiliṉ payaṉi uṟum aḷavāy niṟṟal. e-ṭu: ūrik kākkum 	(to. co. 71 cēṉā. uri)
(iyaṟṟappaṭutal mutaliya mūṉṟiyum vaṭanūlār muṟiyē nirvarttiyam, vikāriyam, pirāppiyam - eṉpa.)
iraṇṭaṉurupu ēṟṟa col teriniliviṉi kuṟippuviṉi iraṇṭaṉiyum avvat toḻiṟpeyarkaḷiyum koṇṭu muṭiyum.
e-ṭu :	marattik kuṟikkum, poruḷi yuṭiyaṉ;
marattik kuṟittal, poruḷi yuṭimi.
ceyappaṭuporuḷ, illatoṉṟu uṇṭākkal (eyili iḻittāṉ) - uḷḷatoṉṟaṉi uṭal vēṟākkal (marattik kuṟittāṉ) - uḷḷatoṉṟu oru toḻil uṟuvittal (kiḷiyi ōppum) - uḷḷatoṉṟaṉi oṉṟu uṟutal (nūl nūṟṟal) - taṉkaṇṇum orutoḻil nikaḻātu ceyappaṭuporuḷ ātal (vāykkālic cārum) - mutalākap palavakippaṭum.
terinilic ceyappaṭuporuḷ - marattik kuṟittāṉ; teriyānilic ceyappaṭuporuḷ - cāttaṉāl maram kuṟikkappaṭṭatu; karuttuḷvaḻic ceyappaṭuporuḷ - cōṟṟi aṭṭāṉ; karuttu ilvaḻic ceyappaṭuporuḷ - cōṟṟik kuḻittāṉ; ceyvāṉum ceyappaṭuporuḷum oṉṟātal - cāttaṉ taṉṉik kuttiṉāṉ; ceyappaṭuporu ḷukku ētuvākiyatu ceyappaṭuporuḷ ātal - kaṟkappaṭum āciriyar, kaṟkappaṭā āciriyar.	(to. co. 73 kal. uri)
oruvaṉ oruviṉi ceyya ataṉāṉ tōṉṟiya poruḷ yātu, atu ceyappaṭuporuḷ eṉpatām. oruvaṉ oruviṉi ceyya attoḻiṟpaṭu poruḷ yātu, atu ceyappaṭu poruḷām. ceyappaṭuporuḷ, ceyporuḷ, karumam, kāriyam - eṉpaṉa oru poruṭkiḷavi.	 (naṉ. 296 caṅ.)
oru viṉimutal ceyyum toḻiliṉi ēṟkum poruḷ yātu, atu ceyappaṭuporuḷām. viṉimutal ceyyum toḻiṟpayaṉ uṟuvatu ceyappaṭuporuḷ eṉṟāl eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ‘cāttaṉ pakiñari veṉṟāṉ’ eṉiṉ, pōrceytal viṉimutaliṉ toḻil; veṟṟiyuṇṭātal ataṉāl varupayaṉ; appayaṉi aṭintavar pakiñar eṉa vēṇṭutaliṉ, atu poruntāmi uṇarka.			(naṉ. 296 irāmā.)
ceyappaṭuporuḷ iruvaki -
taṉkaṇ toḻil nikaḻvatum nikaḻātatum eṉac ceyappaṭu poruḷ iruvakittām. maratti aṟuttāṉ - mutaliyaṉa tam - kaṇ toḻil nikaḻvaṉa; ūrik kāttal - mutaliyaṉa tamkaṇ toḻil nikaḻātaṉa.	(to. co. 73 nac. uri)
ceyappaṭuporuḷ kāraṇakāriyam tōṉṟa niṟpaṉavum, tōṉṟātu niṟpaṉavum - eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
e-ṭu :	āṭiyi neytāṉ, kuṭatti vaṉintāṉ -  kāraṇakāriyam tōṉṟa niṟpaṉa; ūrik kāttāṉ - kāraṇakāriyam tōṉṟātu niṟpatu. (to. co. 70 tey. uri)
ceyappaṭuporuḷ mayakkam -
iraṇṭāmvēṟṟumipporuḷ tōṉṟa avviṭattu ēṉi vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷum vantu mayaṅkuvaṉavām.
cōṟu aṭappaṭṭatu - mutal vēṟṟumi; cōṟṟi aṭṭāṉ - iraṇṭāvatu; ariciyāl cōṟākkiṉāṉ (ariciyi) - mūṉṟāvatu; avaṭkuk koḷḷum ivvaṇikalam (avaḷi) - nāṉkāvatu; paḻiyiṉ añcum (paḻiyi) - aintāvatu; nūlatu kuṟṟam kūṟiṉāṉ (nūli) - āṟāvatu; tūṇiṉkaṇ cārntāṉ (tuṇi) - ēḻāvatu. ivvāṟu ceyappaṭuporuḷ ellā vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷoṭum vantatu.	(i. ko. 45)
ceyappaṭuporuḷ vaki palavātal -
ceyappaṭuporuḷtāṉ, illatoṉṟāy uṇṭākkappaṭu talum, uḷḷatoṉṟāy uṭal vēṟākkutalum, uḷḷa toṉṟāy orutoḻil uṟuvikkappaṭutalum, uḷḷa toṉṟāy oṉṟaṉāṉ uṟappaṭutalum - eṉap palavakittu. ivaṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭu muṟiyē eyili iḻittāṉ - marattik kuṟittāṉ - kiḷiyi ōppum - nūl nūṟṟāṉ - eṉa varum.	(to. co. 73 kal. uri)
ceyappaṭuporuḷ vakiyāka vaṭanūlār kūṟuvaṉa -
niruvarttiyam (nirvartyam), vikāriyam (vikāryam), pirāp piyam (pirāpyam) eṉṉum ivi mūṉṟum vaṭanūlārcilar kūṟum ceyappaṭuporuḷ vakikaḷām. iyaṟṟappaṭuvatu - vēṟupaṭukkappaṭuvatu - eytappaṭuvatu - eṉṟu cēṉāvari yar pōṉṟār kūṟuvaṉavum iviyē. iyaṟṟutalāvatu muṉ illataṉi uṇṭākkutal; vēṟupaṭuttalāvatu muṉ uḷḷataṉip pirittal; eytappaṭutalāvatu ivviraṇṭu miṉṟit toḻiṟpayaṉi uṟuntuṇiyāy niṟṟal - eṉa viritturittu, muṟiyē, eyili iḻikkum - marattik kuṟikkum - putalvarip peṟum - eṉa utāraṇamum kāṭṭuvar cēṉāvariyar. i. ko. nūluṭiyārum ceyappaṭu poruḷ vaki aṉittiyum immūṉṟaṉuḷ ḷum aṭakkuvar. naṉṉūlārum ākkal aḻittal aṭital - eṉat toṭaṅkiyē kūṟuvar.	(pi. vi. 12)
ceyappaṭuporuḷ vēṟuporuḷpaṭutal -
cāttaṉ vīṭṭi virumpiṉāṉ
ceyappaṭuporuḷ eṉpatu karuttā ceyyum viṉiyiṉ payaṉi uṟuvatu. iṅkup payaṉ vīṭu. iccolliṉ poruḷ nalviṉi tīviṉikaḷākiya toḻilkaḷiṉiṉṟu viṭutali peṟutal eṉpatu. eṉavē, vīṭu ceyalaṟṟanili eṉpatu pōtarum. puṭipeyarcciyākiya toḻili nīkkuvatu vīṭu. ātaliṉ ittoṭariṉ ceyappaṭuporuḷ (vīṭu) ceyalaṟṟa niliyākiya vēṟuporuḷpaṭṭatu.
‘urupukaḷ tam poruḷaṉṟi vēṟu poruḷit tarutal’ kāṇka.	(i. ko. 53)
ceyappaṭuporuḷiṉ ēḻvaki -
ceyappaṭuporuḷi ēḻvakiyākak kūṟum ilakkaṇak kottuṭiyār cēṉāvariyar mutalāṉōr kūṟum iyaṟṟappaṭutal - vēṟupaṭukkappaṭutal - eytappaṭutal - eṉṉum mūṉṟu pakuppum tām kūṟum eḻuvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār. ammūvakiyāvaṉa :
iyaṟṟappaṭutal - muṉ illataṉi uṇṭākkutal: e-ṭu : eyili iḻittāṉ; vēṟupaṭuttappaṭutal - vēṟākkap paṭutal : e-ṭu : kayiṟṟi aṟuttāṉ; eytappaṭutal - mēṟ kūṟiya iraṇṭumaṉṟit toḻiṟ payaṉi uṟumtuṇiyāy niṟṟal: e-ṭu : poruḷip peṟṟāṉ.
iṉic ceyappaṭuporuḷiṉ eḻu vakikaḷāvaṉa :
karuttuṇṭātal 	:  cōṟṟi yuṇṭāṉ
karuttu iṉṟātal :  cōṟṟik kuḻittāṉ
irumiyum ātal : eṟumpi mitittu vaḻiyic ceṉṟāṉ; patariyum nelliyum paṇattiṟkuk koṇṭāṉ. (ivaṟṟuḷ eṟumpi mitittalum patarik kōṭalum karuttiṉṟātal; vaḻiyinaṭattalum nellikkōṭalum karuttuṇṭātal.)
īrurupu iṇital	-	pacuviṉip pālikkaṟantāṉ; āriyaṉi aiyuṟṟa poruḷi viṉāviṉāṉ.
karuttāātal - taṉṉip pukaḻntāṉ, taṉṉik kātalit tāṉ.
akaniliātal - varutalic ceytāṉ.
terinili - māṭam ceyyappaṭṭatu - māṭam eḻuvāyāka niṉṟum ceyappaṭuporuḷē eṉa uyttuṇarappaṭṭatu. (terital ārāytal)	(iko. 31)
ceyappaṭuporuḷic ceytatu pōlak kiḷattal -
ceyappaṭuporuḷik karuttāvē pōla vittu ataṉmēl viṉimutalviṉiyi ēṟṟik kūṟutalum vaḻakkiṉkaṇ uṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	immāṭu yāṉ koṇṭatu, iccōṟu yāṉ koṭuttatu, ivveḻuttu yāṉ eḻutiyatu.
iṉi illam meḻukiṟṟu, eḻuttāṇi eḻutiṟṟu - eṉa utāraṇam kāṭṭuvārumuḷar. illam meḻukiṟṟu eṉṉum vaḻakku iṉmiyāṉum, naṭatta naṭanta tēriṉip pōla oruvaṉ eḻuta eḻutiyatu eḻuttāṇiyātaliṉ atu taṉ toḻilē aṉṟi oruvaṉ toḻilaṉṟu ātalāṉum avi kāṭṭu ākā.	(naṉ. 400 caṅ.)
ceyappāṭṭuviṉi vakikaḷ -
ai ceyappāṭṭuviṉi, viṉimutalukkum ceyappaṭu poruḷukkum toḻiṟpeyarkkum payaṉiliyāka nikaḻum. aracaṉ aruccikkappaṭṭāṉ - āṭi tarappaṭṭatu - oḻukkam ceyappaṭum - eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.
‘akaṭārār........... mukaṭiyāṉ mūṭappaṭṭār’  	ku. 936
‘ikaḻap paṭuvārik kāṇiṉum iḻukkē’
‘iṉṉātu irakkap paṭutal’  	ku. 224
eṉpaṉavum avi.
aiai mutaṉili, toḻiṟpeyar, muṟṟu, īreccam, viṉimutal, ceyappaṭuporuḷ - eṉpavaṟṟuḷ paṭucol iṉṟiyum avi paṭuporuḷ tarum.
e-ṭu :	ceykuṉṟu (ceyyappaṭṭa kuṉṟu); mutaṉili paṭucol iṉṟiyum apporuḷ tantatu. ‘āṇṭa vaṉ eṉṟal araṟkē takum’ (eṉṟal - eṉappaṭutal): toḻiṟpeyar paṭucol iṉṟiyum apporuḷ tantatu.
	maram veṭṭiṟṟu (veṭṭappaṭṭatu) muṟṟup paṭucol iṉṟiyum apporuḷ tantatu.
	‘aramporuta poṉ’ ku. 888 (porappaṭṭa): peya reccam paṭucol iṉṟiyum apporuḷ tantatu.
	eḻuti vanta ōli (eḻutappaṭṭu)
	yāḻum eḻuti, muttu eḻuti (kōvi. 79 eḻutap- paṭṭu) viṉiyeccam paṭucol iṉṟiyum ap poruḷ paṭṭatu.
	‘ilvāḻvāṉ eṉpāṉ’ ku. 41 (eṉappaṭuvāṉ):
	‘ūruṇi nirniṟin taṟṟē’ ku. 215 (ūrār nīr uṇṇappaṭuvatu): ceyappaṭuporuḷ paṭucol iṉṟiyum apporuḷ tantatu.
	‘āti’ eṉṟataṉāl, eḻuttāṇi eḻutiṟṟu (eḻut tāṇiyāl eḻutappaṭṭatu) : karuviyiṉ payaṉili paṭucol peṟātu paṭuporuḷ payantatu.
aiaiai. muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ pala paṭucolluṭaṉum varum. mēl virittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa toṭarkaḷē  eṭuttukkāṭ ṭāvaṉa.
aiē. muṟṟuppeyar (viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar), toḻiṟ peyar, īreccam ākiyavaṟṟuḷ paṭucol peṟṟum ap poruḷ peṟātu varum.
‘uṭkappaṭār oḷiyiḻappar........... oḻukuvār’ ku. 921 ‘uṭkār’ eṉavē poruḷpaṭṭatu.
‘peyar eḻuvāy.......... ēṟkap paṭutal’ nēmi. col. 17- ēṟṟal eṉpatē poruḷāyiṟṟu.
uṇṇappaṭṭa cāttaṉ - uṇṭa  cāttaṉ eṉpatē poruḷ.
uṇṇappaṭṭu vanta cāttaṉ  - uṇṭu vanta cāttaṉ eṉpatē poruḷ.
ivi muṟiyē muṟṟuppeyar, toḻiṟpeyar, peyarecca viṉiyeccaṅkaḷām.
ē. peyarukkup piṉṉum viṉiyeccattiṟkup piṉṉum vanta paṭucol ceyappāṭṭuviṉip poruḷpaṭātu vēṟu poruḷ paṭutalumuṇṭu.
‘maṟiiṟantu maṉṟu paṭum’  	ku. 1138
‘pōṟṟiṉum pottup paṭum’  	ku. 468
peyarppiṉ vanta ‘paṭu’ uṇṭām eṉṟu vēṟuporuḷit tantami kāṇka.
‘tuṉpaṅkaḷ ceṉṟu paṭum’ 	ku. 1045
viṉiyeccattiṉ piṉ vanta ‘paṭu’uṇṭām eṉavē vēṟuporuḷpaṭṭatu.
‘iṉitteṉa aṟinta poruṭku ummi paṭum’ 
iṉeṉ urupiṟku iṉeṉ  cāriyi iṉmi paṭum.
peyarccolliṉpiṉ vanta ‘paṭu’ vēṇṭuvatu - takkatu - eṉṉum vēṟuporuḷ tantaṉa.
‘vañcari añcap paṭum’  	ku. 824
‘oḷiyōṭu oḻukap paṭum’  	ku. 698
‘mey collappaṭum’	
viṉiyeccattiṉ piṉ vanta ‘paṭu’ col mēlaṉa pōlavē vēṇṭuvatu - takkatu - eṉṉum vēṟuporuḷ tantaṉa.
ēai. taṉporuḷ piṟaporuḷ iraṇṭaṟkum potuvākavum poruntap paṭucol varum. taṉporuḷ eṉpatu paṭu colloṭu kūṭiya poruḷ; piṟaporuḷ eṉpatu pōnta poruḷām.
‘utavi ceyappaṭṭār’ (ku. 105), iṉmi taḻuvappaṭṭār, allal aṭiyappaṭṭār - ivaṟṟuḷ  mutalām toṭar, utavi ceytavar - utavi peṟṟavar - eṉṉum iruvariyum kuṟikkalām; ēṉiya toṭarkaḷ muṟiyē iṉmi taḻuviṉōriyum, allal aṭintōriyumē kuṟikkum.
eii. paṭucolli varuttiṉum varappaṭāmal taṭippaṭṭuk kiṭappiṉum paṭuporuḷ kuṟippatu. maram veṭṭiṉāṉ, cōṟṟi uṇṭāṉ - ivaṟṟuḷ maram veṭṭappaṭṭatu - cōṟu uṇṇappaṭṭatu - eṉap paṭuporuḷ vantatu.
eiii. paṭucollit taṭuppiṉum taṭippaṭātē vantu paṭuporuḷ taruvatu.
(tokukka muyaṉṟālum tokātu veḷippaṭṭē varutal)
nāy kōṭpaṭṭāṉ, puli kavvappaṭṭāṉ - pōṉṟavaṟṟil taṭuppiṉum taṭipaṭātē paṭuporuḷ vantatu.
io iṉṉum ‘pala’ eṉpataṉāl,
emmāl vīḻappaṭṭa tirunutaṟku illi iṭam, tammāl vīḻappaṭṭa taliviyuṭiya meṉtōḷ - eṉṉumivaṟṟi ‘yām vīḻum tirunutal’ ku. 1123, ‘tām vīḻvār meṉtōḷ’ ku. 1103- eṉat tokukka vēṇṭiṉ tokukkalām, vēṟuporuḷ paṭā tātaliṉ.
‘kēḷviyāl tōṭkappaṭāta cevi’ ku. 418, ‘mukaṭiyāṉ mūṭappaṭṭār’ ku. 936 ivaṟṟik kēḷvi tōḷāta cevi eṉavum mukaṭi mūṭiṉār eṉavum tokukka vēṇṭiṉ tokukkap paṭātu, iraṇṭaṉtokiyāka vēṟuporuḷ paṭutaliṉ.	(i. ko. 78)
